import React from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  TextInput,
  TouchableOpacity,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
} from 'react-native';
import { colors, fonts, radius, spacing } from '../../../constants/theme';
import Icon from '../../ui/Icon';

const CATEGORIES = [
  { key: 'all', label: 'Tất cả' },
  { key: 'fav', label: 'Đã ghim', icon: 'star', iconColor: colors.lemon },
  { key: 'en', label: 'Tiếng Anh', icon: 'translate', iconColor: colors.violet },
  { key: 'ja', label: 'Tiếng Nhật', icon: 'translate', iconColor: colors.coral },
  { key: 'upload', label: 'File máy', icon: 'folder', iconColor: colors.violet },
  { key: 'youtube', label: 'YouTube', dot: '#FF4D4F' },
];

/**
 * Thanh công cụ tìm kiếm, phân loại danh mục và lọc thẻ cho danh sách bài giảng.
 */
export default function HomeFilterBar({
  totalCount = 0,
  onRefresh,
  searchKeyword,
  onChangeSearchKeyword,
  onClearSearchKeyword,
  activeCategory,
  onSelectCategory,
  recentCount = 0,
  favoritesCount = 0,
  selectedFilterTags = [],
  onOpenTagFilterModal,
  onClearTagFilter,
  onRemoveSingleFilterTag,
}) {
  const countFor = (key) => {
    if (key === 'all') return recentCount;
    if (key === 'fav') return favoritesCount;
    return null;
  };

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      {/* Tiêu đề danh sách + Làm mới */}
      <View style={styles.recentHeaderRow}>
        <View style={styles.headerLeft}>
          <Icon name="history" size={19} color={colors.coral} />
          <Text style={styles.sectionHeader}>Bài giảng đã xử lý</Text>
          <View style={styles.countBadge}>
            <Text style={styles.countBadgeText}>{totalCount}</Text>
          </View>
        </View>
        <TouchableOpacity
          onPress={onRefresh}
          style={styles.reloadBtn}
          activeOpacity={0.75}
        >
          <Text style={styles.reloadText}>Làm mới</Text>
          <Icon name="refresh" size={15} color={colors.coral} />
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>

      {/* Tìm kiếm */}
      <View style={styles.searchBarContainer}>
        <Icon name="search" size={19} color={colors.slate} />
        <TextInput
          style={styles.searchInput}
          placeholder="Tìm theo tên bài giảng, tóm tắt, từ khóa..."
          placeholderTextColor="rgba(71, 85, 105, 0.55)"
          value={searchKeyword}
          onChangeText={onChangeSearchKeyword}
          clearButtonMode="while-editing"
        />
        {searchKeyword.trim() ? (
          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={onClearSearchKeyword}
            style={styles.searchClearBtn}
            hitSlop={{ top: 8, bottom: 8, left: 8, right: 8 }}
          >
            <Icon name="close" size={14} color={colors.slate} />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        ) : null}
      </View>

      {/* Bộ lọc danh mục */}
      <ScrollView
        horizontal
        showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
        style={styles.filterChipScroll}
        contentContainerStyle={styles.filterChipContainer}
      >
        {CATEGORIES.map((cat) => {
          const active = activeCategory === cat.key;
          const count = countFor(cat.key);
          return (
            <TouchableOpacity
              key={cat.key}
              style={[styles.filterChip, active && styles.filterChipActive]}
              onPress={() => onSelectCategory(cat.key)}
              activeOpacity={0.8}
            >
              {cat.icon ? (
                <Icon
                  name={cat.icon}
                  size={14}
                  color={active ? '#FFFFFF' : cat.iconColor || colors.slate}
                />
              ) : null}
              {cat.dot ? (
                <View
                  style={[
                    styles.chipDot,
                    { backgroundColor: active ? '#FFFFFF' : cat.dot },
                  ]}
                />
              ) : null}
              <Text
                style={[styles.filterChipText, active && styles.filterChipTextActive]}
              >
                {cat.label}
                {count !== null ? ` (${count})` : ''}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          );
        })}
      </ScrollView>

      {/* Bộ lọc thẻ */}
      <View style={styles.tagFilterBar}>
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[
            styles.tagFilterBtn,
            selectedFilterTags.length > 0 && styles.tagFilterBtnActive,
          ]}
          onPress={onOpenTagFilterModal}
          activeOpacity={0.8}
        >
          <Icon
            name="tag"
            size={16}
            color={selectedFilterTags.length > 0 ? colors.violet : colors.slate}
          />
          <Text
            style={[
              styles.tagFilterBtnText,
              selectedFilterTags.length > 0 && styles.tagFilterBtnTextActive,
            ]}
          >
            {selectedFilterTags.length > 0
              ? `Bộ lọc thẻ (${selectedFilterTags.length})`
              : 'Bộ lọc thẻ'}
          </Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>

        {selectedFilterTags.length > 0 ? (
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.tagClearFilterBtn}
            onPress={onClearTagFilter}
            activeOpacity={0.75}
          >
            <Icon name="close" size={12} color={colors.coralDeep} />
            <Text style={styles.tagClearFilterText}>Hủy lọc</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        ) : null}
      </View>

      {/* Các thẻ đang lọc */}
      {selectedFilterTags.length > 0 ? (
        <View style={styles.activeTagsRow}>
          <Text style={styles.activeTagsLabel}>Đang lọc:</Text>
          <ScrollView
            horizontal
            showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
            contentContainerStyle={styles.activeTagsScroll}
          >
            {selectedFilterTags.map((tag) => (
              <View key={tag} style={styles.activeTagBadge}>
                <Text style={styles.activeTagBadgeText}>#{tag}</Text>
                <TouchableOpacity
                  onPress={() => onRemoveSingleFilterTag(tag)}
                  hitSlop={{ top: 8, bottom: 8, left: 8, right: 8 }}
                >
                  <Icon name="close" size={11} color={colors.violet} />
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>
            ))}
          </ScrollView>
        </View>
      ) : null}
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {
    marginBottom: spacing.sm,
  },

import React from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  TextInput,
  TouchableOpacity,
  ActivityIndicator,
  StyleSheet,
} from 'react-native';
import { colors, fonts, radius, spacing } from '../../../constants/theme';
import Card from '../../ui/Card';
import Icon from '../../ui/Icon';
import IconBadge from '../../ui/IconBadge';
import LanguageMark from '../../ui/LanguageMark';
import GradientButton, { GradientButtonContent } from '../../ui/GradientButton';

/**
 * Section nhập URL hoặc chọn file từ máy, chọn ngôn ngữ đích và các nút bắt đầu.
 * Toàn bộ handler/state được truyền từ HomeScreen, component này chỉ trình bày.
 */
export default function HomeInputSection({
  videoUrl,
  onChangeVideoUrl,
  onClearVideoUrl,
  targetLang,
  onChangeTargetLang,
  supportedLanguages = [],
  loading = false,
  loadingStep = '',
  onProcessVideo,
  onCancelProcessing,
  onPairVideoAndSubtitle,
  onUploadFile,
  onPasteSample,
}) {
  return (
    <Card style={styles.card}>
      <View style={styles.headerRow}>
        <View style={styles.headerLeft}>
          <IconBadge icon="link" size={28} iconSize={15} tone="coral" />
          <Text style={styles.sectionHeader}>HỘP NHẬP LIÊN KẾT BÀI GIẢNG</Text>
        </View>
        <View style={styles.aiReadyPill}>
          <View style={styles.aiReadyDot} />
          <Text style={styles.aiReadyText}>Sẵn sàng</Text>
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* Ô nhập liên kết */}
      <View style={[styles.inputWrapper, loading && styles.inputWrapperDisabled]}>
        <Icon name="video" size={20} color={colors.slate} style={styles.inputIcon} />
        <TextInput
          style={styles.urlInput}
          value={videoUrl}
          onChangeText={onChangeVideoUrl}
          placeholder="Dán đường dẫn YouTube, Drive, LMS..."
          placeholderTextColor="rgba(71, 85, 105, 0.55)"
          autoCapitalize="none"
          autoCorrect={false}
          editable={!loading}
        />
        {videoUrl.length > 0 ? (
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.inlineBtn}
            onPress={onClearVideoUrl}
            activeOpacity={0.75}
            accessibilityLabel="Xóa liên kết"
          >
            <Icon name="close" size={14} color={colors.coral} />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        ) : onPasteSample ? (
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.inlineBtn}
            onPress={onPasteSample}
            activeOpacity={0.75}
          >
            <Text style={styles.inlineBtnText}>Dán mẫu</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        ) : null}
      </View>

      {/* Chọn ngôn ngữ dịch */}
      <View style={styles.langHeaderRow}>
        <Text style={styles.subLabel}>Dịch phụ đề sang ngôn ngữ:</Text>
        <View style={styles.aiLangTag}>
          <Icon name="translate" size={13} color={colors.violet} />
          <Text style={styles.aiLangTagText}>Chuẩn song ngữ</Text>
        </View>
      </View>

      <View style={styles.langRow}>
        {supportedLanguages.map((lang) => {
          const active = targetLang === lang.code;
          return (
            <TouchableOpacity
              key={lang.code}
              style={[styles.langChip, active && styles.activeLangChip]}
              onPress={() => onChangeTargetLang(lang.code)}
              disabled={loading}
              activeOpacity={0.8}
            >
              <Text style={[styles.langChipText, active && styles.activeLangChipText]}>
                {lang.label}
              </Text>
              <LanguageMark code={lang.code} size={16} active={active} />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          );
        })}
      </View>

      {/* Nút hành động chính */}
      <GradientButton
        onPress={onProcessVideo}
        disabled={loading}
        style={styles.primaryAction}
        accessibilityLabel="Bắt đầu tạo phụ đề và tóm tắt"
      >
        {loading ? (
          <View style={styles.loadingBox}>
            <ActivityIndicator color="#FFFFFF" size="small" />
            <Text style={styles.loadingText} numberOfLines={2}>
              {loadingStep || 'Đang xử lý...'}
            </Text>
          </View>
        ) : (
          <GradientButtonContent
            title="BẮT ĐẦU TẠO PHỤ ĐỀ & TÓM TẮT"
            subtitle="Tự động tối ưu: < 30p bóc tách toàn bộ • ≥ 30p nạp ngầm siêu tốc"
            icon={<Icon name="bolt" size={20} color={colors.lemon} />}
          />
        )}
      </GradientButton>

      {loading ? (
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={styles.cancelBtn}
          onPress={onCancelProcessing}
          activeOpacity={0.8}
        >
          <Text style={styles.cancelBtnText}>HỦY TIẾN TRÌNH (GIẢI PHÓNG CPU / RAM)</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      ) : null}

      {/* Hai tác vụ phụ */}
      <View style={styles.quickRow}>
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[styles.quickBtn, loading && styles.disabledBtn]}
          onPress={onPairVideoAndSubtitle}
          disabled={loading}
          activeOpacity={0.8}
        >
          <Icon name="merge" size={18} color={colors.coral} />
          <Text style={styles.quickBtnText} numberOfLines={1}>
            Ghép đôi phụ đề
          </Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>

        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[styles.quickBtn, styles.quickBtnMint, loading && styles.disabledBtn]}
          onPress={onUploadFile}
          disabled={loading}
          activeOpacity={0.8}
        >
          <Icon name="upload" size={18} color={colors.mintDeep} />
          <Text style={[styles.quickBtnText, styles.quickBtnTextMint]} numberOfLines={1}>
            Tải file MP4/MP3
          </Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>
    </Card>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  card: {
    marginBottom: spacing.md,
  },
  headerRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    gap: spacing.sm,
    marginBottom: spacing.sm + 2,
  },

import React from 'react';
import { View, Text, StyleSheet } from 'react-native';
import { colors, fonts, spacing } from '../../../constants/theme';
import Card from '../../ui/Card';
import Icon from '../../ui/Icon';
import IconBadge from '../../ui/IconBadge';

const METRICS = [
  { key: 'lectures', label: 'Bài giảng', icon: 'library', tone: 'mint', color: colors.mintDeep },
  { key: 'minutes', label: 'Thời gian học', icon: 'clock', tone: 'violet', color: colors.violet, suffix: 'ph' },
  { key: 'quizzes', label: 'Câu trắc nghiệm', icon: 'quiz', tone: 'coral', color: colors.coralDeep },
];

/**
 * Khối thống kê tổng quan tiến độ học tập (Bài giảng, Phút học, Câu Quiz).
 */
export default function HomeStatsSection({
  totalLectures = 0,
  totalMinutes = 0,
  totalQuizzes = 0,
}) {
  const values = {
    lectures: totalLectures,
    minutes: totalMinutes,
    quizzes: totalQuizzes,
  };

  return (
    <View style={styles.section}>
      <View style={styles.headerRow}>
        <View style={styles.headerLeft}>
          <Icon name="stats" size={19} color={colors.violet} />
          <Text style={styles.sectionHeader}>THỐNG KÊ TIẾN ĐỘ HỌC TẬP</Text>
        </View>
        <Text style={styles.headerMeta}>Tuần này</Text>
      </View>

      <View style={styles.statsRow}>
        {METRICS.map((metric) => (
          <Card key={metric.key} style={styles.statBox} tone={metric.tone} padded={false}>
            <View style={styles.statInner}>
              <IconBadge
                icon={metric.icon}
                tone={metric.tone}
                size={30}
                iconSize={16}
                style={styles.statIcon}
              />
              <View style={styles.valueRow}>
                <Text style={[styles.statNumber, { color: metric.color }]}>
                  {values[metric.key]}
                </Text>
                {metric.suffix ? (
                  <Text style={[styles.statSuffix, { color: metric.color }]}>{metric.suffix}</Text>
                ) : null}
              </View>
              <Text style={styles.statLabel} numberOfLines={1}>
                {metric.label}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </Card>
        ))}
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  section: {
    marginBottom: spacing.md,
  },
  headerRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    paddingHorizontal: 4,
    marginBottom: 10,
  },
  headerLeft: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    gap: 6,
  },
  sectionHeader: {
    fontFamily: fonts.bold,
    fontSize: 11,
    lineHeight: 15,
    letterSpacing: 0.6,
    color: colors.ink,
  },
  headerMeta: {
    fontFamily: fonts.medium,
    fontSize: 10.5,
    lineHeight: 14,
    color: colors.muted,
  },

    flexDirection: 'row',
    gap: 10,
  },
  statBox: {
    flex: 1,

    alignItems: 'center',

FILE: src/mobile-app/src/components/home/sections/HomeWelcomeSection.js

import React from 'react';
import { View, Text, StyleSheet } from 'react-native';
import { colors, fonts, radius, spacing } from '../../../constants/theme';
import Card from '../../ui/Card';
import Icon from '../../ui/Icon';

const DAILY_GOAL_MINUTES = 20;

/**
 * Banner chào mừng + mục tiêu học tập trong ngày.
 * Số phút lấy từ dữ liệu thật (tổng thời lượng bài giảng đã xử lý).
 */
export default function HomeWelcomeSection({
  totalMinutes = 0,
  streakDays = 1,
}) {
  const doneMinutes = Math.max(0, Math.round(totalMinutes));
  const percent = Math.min(100, Math.round((doneMinutes / DAILY_GOAL_MINUTES) * 100));

  return (
    <Card style={styles.card} padded={false}>
      <View pointerEvents="none" style={styles.decorCircle} />
      <View pointerEvents="none" style={styles.decorCircleSmall} />

      <View style={styles.content}>
        <View style={styles.topRow}>
          <View style={styles.greetingRow}>
            <View style={styles.emojiCircle}>
              <Icon name="sparkle" size={17} color={colors.coral} />
            </View>
            <View style={styles.greetingTextWrap}>
              <Text style={styles.title}>Chào mừng trở lại!</Text>
              <Text style={styles.subtitle}>Sẵn sàng khám phá kiến thức mới hôm nay</Text>
            </View>
          </View>

          <View style={styles.streakPill}>
            <Icon name="fire" size={12} color={colors.coralDeep} />
            <Text style={styles.streakText}>{streakDays} ngày</Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.goalBox}>
          <View style={styles.goalLeft}>
            <View style={styles.goalIcon}>
              <Icon name="check" size={17} color={colors.mintDeep} />
            </View>
            <View style={styles.goalContent}>
              <Text style={styles.goalLabel} numberOfLines={1}>
                Mục tiêu ngày: {doneMinutes} / {DAILY_GOAL_MINUTES} phút
              </Text>
              <View style={styles.track}>
                <View style={[styles.fill, { width: `${percent}%` }]} />
              </View>
            </View>
          </View>

          <View style={styles.percentPill}>
            <Text style={styles.percentText}>{percent}%</Text>
          </View>
        </View>
      </View>
    </Card>
  );
}

export const LANG_MAP = {
  vi: { code: 'vi', name: 'Tiếng Việt' },
  en: { code: 'en', name: 'Tiếng Anh' },
  ja: { code: 'ja', name: 'Tiếng Nhật' },
  ko: { code: 'ko', name: 'Tiếng Hàn' },
  zh: { code: 'zh', name: 'Tiếng Trung' },
  fr: { code: 'fr', name: 'Tiếng Pháp' },
  de: { code: 'de', name: 'Tiếng Đức' },
  es: { code: 'es', name: 'Tiếng Tây Ban Nha' },
  ru: { code: 'ru', name: 'Tiếng Nga' },
};

export const getLangMeta = (code, fallbackName = 'Ngôn ngữ') => {
  if (!code) return { code: '', name: fallbackName };
  const clean = String(code).toLowerCase().trim();
  return LANG_MAP[clean] || { code: clean, name: clean.toUpperCase() };
};

// HTML nhúng YouTube Iframe API cho Mobile WebView (Tương thích Android / iOS 100%)
export const generateYouTubeHtml = (youtubeId, startSeconds = 0) => `
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0, maximum-scale=1.0, user-scalable=no">
  <style>
    * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }
    html, body { width: 100%; height: 100%; background: #000; overflow: hidden; }
    #player { width: 100%; height: 100%; position: absolute; top: 0; left: 0; }
    video::-webkit-media-controls { opacity: 0.99 !important; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <div id="player"></div>
  <script>
    var tag = document.createElement('script');
    tag.src = "https://www.youtube.com/iframe_api";
    var firstScriptTag = document.getElementsByTagName('script')[0];
    firstScriptTag.parentNode.insertBefore(tag, firstScriptTag);

    var player;
    function onYouTubeIframeAPIReady() {
      player = new YT.Player('player', {
        height: '100%',
        width: '100%',
        videoId: '${youtubeId}',
        playerVars: {
          playsinline: 1,
          controls: 1,
          rel: 0,
          showinfo: 0,
          modestbranding: 1,
          enablejsapi: 1,
          fs: 1,
          start: ${Math.floor(startSeconds || 0)},
          origin: 'https://www.youtube.com'
        },
        events: {
          'onReady': onPlayerReady,
          'onStateChange': onPlayerStateChange
        }
      });
      window.player = player;
    }

    function post(type, data) {
      if (window.ReactNativeWebView && window.ReactNativeWebView.postMessage) {
        window.ReactNativeWebView.postMessage(JSON.stringify(Object.assign({ type: type }, data || {})));
      }
    }

    var timer = null;
    function onPlayerReady(event) {
      window.player = player;
      post('ready', { duration: player.getDuration ? player.getDuration() : 0 });
    }

    function onPlayerStateChange(event) {
      post('stateChange', { state: event.data });
      if (event.data === 1) {
        if (!timer) {
          timer = setInterval(function() {
            if (player && player.getCurrentTime) {
              post('timeUpdate', { time: player.getCurrentTime() });
            }
          }, 250);
        }
      } else {
        if (timer) {
          clearInterval(timer);
          timer = null;
        }
      }
    }

    window.addEventListener('message', function(e) { handleCommand(e.data); });
    document.addEventListener('message', function(e) { handleCommand(e.data); });
    function handleCommand(raw) {
      try {
        var data = typeof raw === 'string' ? JSON.parse(raw) : raw;
        if (!player) return;
        if (data.action === 'seek' && player.seekTo) {
          player.seekTo(data.time, true);
          if (player.playVideo) player.playVideo();
        } else if (data.action === 'pause' && player.pauseVideo) {
          player.pauseVideo();
        } else if (data.action === 'play' && player.playVideo) {
          player.playVideo();
        }
      } catch(e) {}
    }
  </script>
</body>
</html>
`;

// HTML nhúng Local Media/Audio cho Mobile WebView
export const generateMediaHtml = (src, isAudio = false, startSeconds = 0) => `
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0, maximum-scale=1.0, user-scalable=no">
  <style>
    * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }
    html, body { width: 100%; height: 100%; background: #000; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; overflow: hidden; }
    video, audio { width: 100%; height: 100%; max-height: 100%; object-fit: contain; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <${isAudio ? 'audio' : 'video'} id="media" src="${src}" controls playsinline preload="auto"></${isAudio ? 'audio' : 'video'}>
  <script>
    var media = document.getElementById('media');
    function post(type, data) {
      if (window.ReactNativeWebView && window.ReactNativeWebView.postMessage) {
        window.ReactNativeWebView.postMessage(JSON.stringify(Object.assign({ type: type }, data || {})));
      }
    }
    media.addEventListener('loadedmetadata', function() {
      post('ready', { duration: media.duration });
      if (${Math.floor(startSeconds || 0)} > 0) {
        media.currentTime = ${Math.floor(startSeconds || 0)};
      }
    });
    media.addEventListener('play', function() { post('stateChange', { state: 1 }); });
    media.addEventListener('pause', function() { post('stateChange', { state: 2 }); });
    media.addEventListener('timeupdate', function() {
      post('timeUpdate', { time: media.currentTime });
    });

    window.addEventListener('message', function(e) { handleCommand(e.data); });
    document.addEventListener('message', function(e) { handleCommand(e.data); });
    function handleCommand(raw) {
      try {
        var data = typeof raw === 'string' ? JSON.parse(raw) : raw;
        if (!media) return;
        if (data.action === 'seek') {
          media.currentTime = data.time;
          media.play();
        } else if (data.action === 'pause') {
          media.pause();
        } else if (data.action === 'play') {
          media.play();
        }
      } catch(e) {}
    }
  </script>
</body>
</html>
`;

FILE: src/mobile-app/src/components/player/sections/InVideoQuizOverlay.js

import React from 'react';
import { View, Text, TouchableOpacity } from 'react-native';
import Icon from '../../ui/Icon';
import { colors } from '../../../constants/theme';

export default function InVideoQuizOverlay({
  activeCheckpointQuiz,
  formatTime,
  handleResumeFromQuiz,
  handleSelectQuizOption,
  styles,
}) {
  if (!activeCheckpointQuiz) return null;

  return (
    <View style={styles.checkpointOverlay}>
      <View style={styles.checkpointCard}>
        <View style={styles.checkpointTopRow}>
          <View style={styles.checkpointBadge}>
            <Icon name="quiz" size={11} color={colors.coralDeep} />
            <Text style={styles.checkpointBadgeText}>
              ĐIỂM ÔN TẬP • MỐC {formatTime(activeCheckpointQuiz.quiz.checkpoint_time)}
            </Text>
          </View>
          {!activeCheckpointQuiz.isSubmitted && (
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.checkpointSkipBtn}
              onPress={handleResumeFromQuiz}
              activeOpacity={0.7}
            >
              <Text style={styles.checkpointSkipText}>Bỏ qua</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          )}
        </View>

        <Text style={styles.checkpointQuestion}>
          {activeCheckpointQuiz.quiz.question}
        </Text>

        <View style={styles.checkpointOptionsList}>
          {activeCheckpointQuiz.quiz.options.map((opt, oIdx) => {
            const isChosen = activeCheckpointQuiz.userChoice === opt;
            const correctAns = (
              activeCheckpointQuiz.quiz.correct_answer ||
              activeCheckpointQuiz.quiz.answer ||
              ''
            ).toUpperCase().trim();
            const isCorrectOption =
              opt.toUpperCase().startsWith(correctAns[0]) ||
              opt.toUpperCase() === correctAns;

            let btnStyle = styles.checkpointOptionBtn;
            if (activeCheckpointQuiz.isSubmitted) {
              if (isCorrectOption) {
                btnStyle = [styles.checkpointOptionBtn, styles.checkpointOptionCorrect];
              } else if (isChosen && !activeCheckpointQuiz.isCorrect) {
                btnStyle = [styles.checkpointOptionBtn, styles.checkpointOptionWrong];
              }
            } else if (isChosen) {
              btnStyle = [styles.checkpointOptionBtn, styles.checkpointOptionSelected];
            }

            return (
              <TouchableOpacity
                key={oIdx}
                style={btnStyle}
                disabled={activeCheckpointQuiz.isSubmitted}
                onPress={() => handleSelectQuizOption(opt)}
                activeOpacity={0.7}
              >
                <Text style={styles.checkpointOptionText}>{opt}</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            );
          })}
        </View>

        {activeCheckpointQuiz.isSubmitted && (
          <View style={styles.checkpointFeedbackBox}>
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.checkpointFeedbackTitle,
                activeCheckpointQuiz.isCorrect
                  ? styles.checkpointFeedbackCorrect
                  : styles.checkpointFeedbackWrong,
              ]}
            >
              {activeCheckpointQuiz.isCorrect
                ? '✓ Chính xác! Bạn nắm bài rất tốt.'
                : '✗ Chưa đúng! Xem giải thích bên dưới:'}
            </Text>
            <Text style={styles.checkpointExplanation}>
              {activeCheckpointQuiz.quiz.explanation}
            </Text>
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.checkpointContinueBtn}
              onPress={handleResumeFromQuiz}
              activeOpacity={0.8}
            >
              <Text style={styles.checkpointContinueBtnText}>
                TIẾP TỤC XEM BÀI GIẢNG
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        )}
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

FILE: src/mobile-app/src/components/player/sections/PlayerControlsSection.js
================================================
import React from 'react';
import { View, Text, TouchableOpacity, Platform } from 'react-native';
import Icon from '../../ui/Icon';
import { colors } from '../../../constants/theme';

export default function PlayerControlsSection({
  isSeeking,
  seekDragTime,
  currentTime,
  duration,
  isPlaying,
  showCC,
  formatTime,
  handleSeek,
  togglePlayPause,
  setShowCC,
  setIsSeeking,
  setSeekDragTime,
  seekBarLayoutRef,
  styles,
}) {
  return (
    <>
      {/* Thanh Kéo Tua Tiến Trình Bài Giảng (Chỉ hiển thị trên Web, tự động ẩn trên Máy ảo / APK điện thoại) */}
      {Platform.OS === 'web' && (
        <View
          style={styles.seekBarWrap}
          onLayout={(e) => {
            const { width, x } = e.nativeEvent.layout;
            if (seekBarLayoutRef) seekBarLayoutRef.current = { width, x };
          }}
          onStartShouldSetResponder={() => true}
          onMoveShouldSetResponder={() => true}
          onResponderGrant={(e) => {
            const touchX = e.nativeEvent.locationX;
            const barWidth = seekBarLayoutRef?.current?.width || 1;
            const ratio = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, touchX / barWidth));
            const targetTime = ratio * duration;
            setIsSeeking(true);
            setSeekDragTime(targetTime);
          }}
          onResponderMove={(e) => {
            const touchX = e.nativeEvent.locationX;
            const barWidth = seekBarLayoutRef?.current?.width || 1;
            const ratio = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, touchX / barWidth));
            const targetTime = ratio * duration;
            setSeekDragTime(targetTime);
          }}
          onResponderRelease={(e) => {
            const touchX = e.nativeEvent.locationX;
            const barWidth = seekBarLayoutRef?.current?.width || 1;
            const ratio = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, touchX / barWidth));
            const targetTime = ratio * duration;
            setIsSeeking(false);
            handleSeek(targetTime);
          }}
        >
          {/* Tooltip mốc thời gian nổi khi đang kéo trượt */}
          {isSeeking && (
            <View
              style={[
                styles.seekTooltip,
                {
                  left: `${Math.max(4, Math.min(90, (seekDragTime / (duration || 1)) * 100))}%`,
                },
              ]}
              pointerEvents="none"
            >
              <Text style={styles.seekTooltipText}>{formatTime(seekDragTime)}</Text>
            </View>
          )}

          {/* Thanh ray nền */}
          <View style={styles.seekBarTrack}>
            <View
              style={[
                styles.seekBarFill,
                {
                  width: `${Math.max(0, Math.min(100, ((isSeeking ? seekDragTime : currentTime) / (duration || 1)) * 100))}%`,
                },
              ]}
            />
          </View>

          {/* Nút tròn kéo tua (Scrubber Thumb) */}
          <View
            style={[
              styles.seekBarThumb,
              {
                left: `${Math.max(0, Math.min(100, ((isSeeking ? seekDragTime : currentTime) / (duration || 1)) * 100))}%`,
              },
            ]}
            pointerEvents="none"
          />
        </View>
      )}

      {/* Thanh Trạng thái & Điều khiển Phát / Tạm dừng & Tua */}
      <View style={styles.realtimeStatusBar}>
        {Platform.OS === 'web' ? (
          <View style={styles.controlsLeftGroup}>
            {/* Nút Play / Pause */}
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[styles.playControlBtn, isPlaying && styles.playControlBtnActive]}
              onPress={togglePlayPause}
              activeOpacity={0.7}
            >
              <Icon
                name={isPlaying ? 'pause' : 'play'}
                size={14}
                color="#FFFFFF"
              />
              <Text style={styles.playControlText}>
                {isPlaying ? 'Tạm dừng' : 'Phát'}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            {/* Nút tua lùi 5s */}
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.quickSeekBtn}
              onPress={() => handleSeek(currentTime - 5)}
              activeOpacity={0.7}
              accessibilityLabel="Tua lùi 5 giây"
            >
              <Icon name="replay5" size={18} color={colors.slate} />
            </TouchableOpacity>

            {/* Nút tua tới 5s */}
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.quickSeekBtn}
              onPress={() => handleSeek(currentTime + 5)}
              activeOpacity={0.7}
              accessibilityLabel="Tua tới 5 giây"
            >
              <Icon name="forward5" size={18} color={colors.slate} />
            </TouchableOpacity>

            {/* Nút bật/tắt phụ đề nổi */}
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[styles.ccBtn, showCC && styles.ccBtnActive]}
              onPress={() => setShowCC(!showCC)}
              activeOpacity={0.7}
            >
              <Icon
                name="closed-caption"
                size={15}
                color={showCC ? '#FFFFFF' : colors.slate}
              />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        ) : (
          /* Trên Mobile (Máy ảo / APK): Ẩn các nút điều khiển thừa, chỉ hiện chấm trạng thái tinh tế */
          <View style={styles.liveIndicator}>
            <View style={[styles.liveDot, isPlaying && styles.liveDotActive]} />
            <Text style={styles.liveText}>
              {isPlaying ? 'ĐANG PHÁT' : 'TẠM DỪNG'}
            </Text>
          </View>
        )}

        <View style={styles.statusRightGroup}>
          <Text style={styles.liveTimerText}>
            {formatTime(isSeeking ? seekDragTime : currentTime)} / {formatTime(duration)}
          </Text>
        </View>
      </View>
    </>
  );
}

import React from 'react';
import { View, Text, ScrollView, TouchableOpacity, TextInput } from 'react-native';
import SubtitleItem from '../../SubtitleItem';
import Icon from '../../ui/Icon';
import LanguageMark from '../../ui/LanguageMark';
import { spacing, colors } from '../../../constants/theme';

export default function SubtitleListSection({
  scrollRef,
  hasDifferentOriginal,
  sourceLangCode,
  targetLangCode,
  sourceLangInfo,
  targetLangInfo,
  subMode,
  setSubMode,
  interactiveQuizEnabled,
  setInteractiveQuizEnabled,
  checkpointQuizzes,
  streamProgress,
  searchQuery,
  setSearchQuery,
  filteredSegments,
  segments,
  activeSegmentIndex,
  handleSeek,
  styles,
}) {
  return (
    <ScrollView
      ref={scrollRef}
      style={styles.subtitlesScroll}
      contentContainerStyle={styles.subtitlesScrollContent}
      showsVerticalScrollIndicator={true}
      keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
    >
      {/* Thanh chọn chế độ Ngôn ngữ Phụ đề */}
      <View style={styles.langModeBar}>
        <Text style={styles.langModeLabel}>Phụ đề:</Text>
        <View style={styles.langModeButtons}>
          {/* Nút 1: Chế độ Song ngữ (nếu có bản dịch khác tiếng gốc) */}
          {(hasDifferentOriginal || sourceLangCode !== targetLangCode) && (
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[
                styles.langModeBtn,
                subMode === 'bilingual' && styles.langModeBtnActive,
              ]}
              onPress={() => setSubMode('bilingual')}
              activeOpacity={0.7}
            >
              <Icon
                name="translate"
                size={12}
                color={subMode === 'bilingual' ? '#FFFFFF' : colors.slate}
              />
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.langModeBtnText,
                  subMode === 'bilingual' && styles.langModeBtnTextActive,
                ]}
              >
                Song ngữ
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          )}

          {/* Nút 2: Phụ đề đã dịch (Target Language) */}
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[
              styles.langModeBtn,
              subMode === 'target' && styles.langModeBtnActive,
            ]}
            onPress={() => setSubMode('target')}
            activeOpacity={0.7}
          >
            <LanguageMark code={targetLangCode} size={14} active={subMode === 'target'} />
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.langModeBtnText,
                subMode === 'target' && styles.langModeBtnTextActive,
              ]}
            >
              {targetLangInfo.name}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>

          {/* Nút 3: Ngôn ngữ gốc người nói trong video */}
          {(hasDifferentOriginal || sourceLangCode !== targetLangCode) && (
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[
                styles.langModeBtn,
                subMode === 'original' && styles.langModeBtnActive,
              ]}
              onPress={() => setSubMode('original')}
              activeOpacity={0.7}
            >
              <LanguageMark code={sourceLangCode} size={14} active={subMode === 'original'} />
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.langModeBtnText,
                  subMode === 'original' && styles.langModeBtnTextActive,
                ]}
              >
                {sourceLangInfo.name} (Gốc)
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          )}
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* Thanh tùy chọn Trắc nghiệm tương tác theo mốc video */}
      <View style={styles.interactiveQuizBar}>
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[
            styles.quizTogglePill,
            interactiveQuizEnabled && styles.quizTogglePillActive,
          ]}
          onPress={() => setInteractiveQuizEnabled(!interactiveQuizEnabled)}
          activeOpacity={0.8}
        >
          <Icon
            name={interactiveQuizEnabled ? 'quiz' : 'pause'}
            size={14}
            color={colors.mintDeep}
          />
          <Text
            style={[
              styles.quizTogglePillText,
              interactiveQuizEnabled && styles.quizTogglePillTextActive,
            ]}
          >
            Trắc nghiệm theo video: {interactiveQuizEnabled ? 'ĐANG BẬT' : 'ĐÃ TẮT'}
          </Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>

        {checkpointQuizzes.length > 0 && (
          <View style={styles.checkpointCountBadge}>
            <Text style={styles.checkpointCountText}>
              {checkpointQuizzes.length} mốc ôn tập
            </Text>
          </View>
        )}
      </View>

      {/* Banner thông báo tiến độ nạp ngầm song song cho video dài (Giai đoạn 3) */}
      {streamProgress !== null && streamProgress < 100 && (
        <View style={styles.streamingBanner}>
          <Icon name="bolt" size={13} color={colors.violet} />
          <Text style={styles.streamingBannerText}>
            Đang xử lý ngầm và nạp dần phụ đề ({streamProgress}%)... Bạn vẫn theo dõi video bình thường.
          </Text>
        </View>
      )}

      {/* Thanh tìm kiếm phụ đề và mốc thời gian */}
      <View style={styles.searchBar}>
        <Icon name="search" size={16} color={colors.muted} style={{ marginRight: 8 }} />
        <TextInput
          style={styles.searchInput}
          placeholder="Tìm câu / chủ đề bài giảng (ví dụ: công thức, khái niệm)..."
          placeholderTextColor={colors.textMuted}
          value={searchQuery}
          onChangeText={setSearchQuery}
        />
        {searchQuery.length > 0 && (
          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={() => setSearchQuery('')}
            style={styles.clearSearchBtn}
          >
            <Icon name="close" size={14} color={colors.muted} />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        )}
      </View>

      {/* Thống kê kết quả tìm kiếm */}
      {searchQuery.trim().length > 0 && (
        <View style={styles.searchStatsBar}>
          <Text style={styles.searchStatsText}>
            Tìm thấy <Text style={{ color: '#f59e0b', fontWeight: '800' }}>{filteredSegments.length}</Text> câu phụ đề khớp với "{searchQuery.trim()}":
          </Text>
        </View>
      )}

      {/* Danh sách phụ đề đồng bộ chạy theo giây */}
      <View style={{ paddingHorizontal: spacing.md, paddingTop: 4 }}>
        {filteredSegments.length > 0 ? (
          filteredSegments.map((seg, idx) => (
            <SubtitleItem
              key={idx}
              segment={seg}
              isActive={segments[activeSegmentIndex] === seg}
              onSeek={handleSeek}
              subMode={subMode}
              searchQuery={searchQuery}
            />
          ))
        ) : (
          <View style={styles.emptyBox}>
            <Text style={styles.emptyText}>
              {searchQuery
                ? `Không tìm thấy câu nào khớp với "${searchQuery}"`
                : 'Chưa có phụ đề bóc tách cho bài giảng này.'}
            </Text>
          </View>
        )}
      </View>
    </ScrollView>
  );
}

FILE: src/mobile-app/src/components/player/sections/VideoPlayerSection.js
================================================
import React from 'react';
import { View, Text, Platform, Image } from 'react-native';
import { WebView } from 'react-native-webview';
import YoutubePlayer from 'react-native-youtube-iframe';
import InVideoQuizOverlay from './InVideoQuizOverlay';
import Icon from '../../ui/Icon';

export default function VideoPlayerSection({
  isYouTube,
  youtubeId,
  mediaSrc,
  isAudioOnly,
  title,
  duration,
  effectiveSeekTime,
  isPlaying,
  setIsPlaying,
  htmlMediaRef,
  ytPlayerRef,
  webViewRef,
  lastTimeUpdateRef,
  lecture,
  handleMediaLoadedMetadata,
  handleMediaTimeUpdate,
  handleMediaSeeked,
  handleWebViewMessage,
  showCC,
  activeSegment,
  subMode,
  activeCheckpointQuiz,
  formatTime,
  handleResumeFromQuiz,
  handleSelectQuizOption,
  generateMediaHtml,
  apiService,
  styles,
}) {
  return (
    <View style={styles.videoContainer}>
      {Platform.OS === 'web' ? (
        isYouTube && youtubeId ? (
          <iframe
            id="yt-sync-iframe"
            src={`https://www.youtube.com/embed/${youtubeId}?enablejsapi=1&origin=${encodeURIComponent(typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.location.origin : '')}`}
            style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', minHeight: 220, border: 'none', borderRadius: 12 }}
            allow="accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture"
            allowFullScreen
            title={title}
          />
        ) : mediaSrc ? (
          !isAudioOnly ? (
            <video
              ref={htmlMediaRef}
              src={mediaSrc}
              controls
              playsInline
              preload="auto"
              style={{
                width: '100%',
                height: '100%',
                minHeight: 220,
                maxHeight: 260,
                backgroundColor: '#000',
                borderRadius: 12,
                objectFit: 'contain',
              }}
              onLoadedMetadata={handleMediaLoadedMetadata}
              onTimeUpdate={handleMediaTimeUpdate}
              onSeeked={handleMediaSeeked}
              onPlay={() => setIsPlaying(true)}
              onPause={() => setIsPlaying(false)}
            />
          ) : (
            <div
              style={{
                display: 'flex',
                flexDirection: 'column',
                justifyContent: 'center',
                alignItems: 'center',
                padding: '24px 16px',
                backgroundColor: '#0f172a',
                borderRadius: 12,
                width: '100%',
                height: '100%',
                minHeight: 200,
                boxSizing: 'border-box',
              }}
            >
              <div
                style={{
                  width: 56,
                  height: 56,
                  borderRadius: 28,
                  backgroundColor: 'rgba(255, 111, 97, 0.16)',
                  display: 'flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  justifyContent: 'center',
                  marginBottom: 10,
                }}
              >
                <svg
                  width="26"
                  height="26"
                  viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                  fill="none"
                  stroke="#FF8A65"
                  strokeWidth="1.8"
                  strokeLinecap="round"
                  strokeLinejoin="round"
                >
                  <path d="M12 3a3 3 0 0 0-3 3v6a3 3 0 0 0 6 0V6a3 3 0 0 0-3-3z" />
                  <path d="M19 11a7 7 0 0 1-14 0" />
                  <line x1="12" y1="18" x2="12" y2="21" />
                  <line x1="8.5" y1="21" x2="15.5" y2="21" />
                </svg>
              </div>
              <div
                style={{
                  color: '#f8fafc',
                  fontSize: 14,
                  fontWeight: '600',
                  marginBottom: 16,
                  textAlign: 'center',
                  maxWidth: '90%',
                  overflow: 'hidden',
                  textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
                  whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                }}
              >
                {title}
              </div>
              <audio
                ref={htmlMediaRef}
                src={mediaSrc}
                controls
                preload="auto"
                style={{ width: '100%', maxWidth: 400 }}
                onLoadedMetadata={handleMediaLoadedMetadata}
                onTimeUpdate={handleMediaTimeUpdate}
                onSeeked={handleMediaSeeked}
                onPlay={() => setIsPlaying(true)}
                onPause={() => setIsPlaying(false)}
              />
            </div>
          )
        ) : youtubeId ? (
          <View style={styles.thumbnailContainer}>
            <Image
              source={{ uri: `https://img.youtube.com/vi/${youtubeId}/hqdefault.jpg` }}
              style={styles.thumbnailImage}
              resizeMode="cover"
            />
            <View style={styles.thumbnailOverlay}>
              <Text style={styles.thumbnailTitle} numberOfLines={2}>
                {title}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        ) : (
          <View style={styles.mediaPlaceholder}>
            <View style={styles.mediaIconBadge}>
              <Icon name="school" size={26} color="#FFFFFF" />
            </View>
            <Text style={styles.mediaTitle} numberOfLines={2}>
              {title}
            </Text>
            <Text style={styles.mediaStatus}>
              Bài giảng mẫu • {formatTime(duration)}
            </Text>
          </View>
        )
      ) : (
        /* PHÁT TRÊN MOBILE (Android / iOS) QUA REACT-NATIVE-YOUTUBE-IFRAME */
        isYouTube && youtubeId ? (
          <YoutubePlayer
            ref={ytPlayerRef}
            height={220}
            play={isPlaying}
            videoId={youtubeId}
            initialPlayerParams={{
              preventFullScreen: false,
              controls: true,
              rel: false,
              start: Math.floor(effectiveSeekTime || 0),
            }}
            onReady={() => {
              if (effectiveSeekTime > 0 && ytPlayerRef.current?.seekTo) {
                try {
                  ytPlayerRef.current.seekTo(effectiveSeekTime, true);
                } catch (_) {}
              }
            }}
            onChangeState={(state) => {
              if (state === 'playing') {
                setIsPlaying(true);
              } else if (state === 'paused') {
                setIsPlaying(false);
                if (lecture?.video_url && lastTimeUpdateRef?.current > 0) {
                  apiService.savePlaybackProgress(
                    lecture.video_url,
                    lastTimeUpdateRef.current,
                    duration
                  );
                }
              } else if (state === 'ended') {
                setIsPlaying(false);
              }
            }}
            webViewProps={{
              androidLayerType: 'hardware',
              androidHardwareAccelerationDisabled: false,
              setSupportMultipleWindows: false,
            }}
          />
        ) : mediaSrc ? (
          <WebView
            ref={webViewRef}
            style={styles.nativeWebView}
            containerStyle={styles.nativeWebViewContainer}
            originWhitelist={['*']}
            source={{
              html: generateMediaHtml(mediaSrc, isAudioOnly, effectiveSeekTime),
            }}
            userAgent="Mozilla/5.0 (Linux; Android 10; K) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/128.0.0.0 Mobile Safari/537.36"
            allowsInlineMediaPlayback={true}
            allowsFullscreenVideo={true}
            mediaPlaybackRequiresUserAction={false}
            mediaPlaybackRequiresUserGesture={false}
            javaScriptEnabled={true}
            domStorageEnabled={true}
            scrollEnabled={false}
            androidLayerType="hardware"
            androidHardwareAccelerationDisabled={false}
            onMessage={handleWebViewMessage}
          />
        ) : youtubeId ? (
          <View style={styles.thumbnailContainer}>
            <Image
              source={{ uri: `https://img.youtube.com/vi/${youtubeId}/hqdefault.jpg` }}
              style={styles.thumbnailImage}
              resizeMode="cover"
            />
            <View style={styles.thumbnailOverlay}>
              <Text style={styles.thumbnailTitle} numberOfLines={2}>
                {title}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        ) : (
          <View style={styles.mediaPlaceholder}>
            <View style={styles.mediaIconBadge}>
              <Icon name="school" size={26} color="#FFFFFF" />
            </View>
            <Text style={styles.mediaTitle} numberOfLines={2}>
              {title}
            </Text>
            <Text style={styles.mediaStatus}>
              Bài giảng mẫu • {formatTime(duration)}
            </Text>
          </View>
        )
      )}

      {/* Lớp phủ phụ đề nổi trên video (Overlay CC) */}
      {showCC && activeSegment && !activeCheckpointQuiz && (
        <View style={styles.ccOverlayContainer} pointerEvents="none">
          <View style={styles.ccOverlayBox}>
            <Text style={styles.ccOverlayText} numberOfLines={2} ellipsizeMode="tail">
              {subMode === 'original'
                ? activeSegment.original_text || activeSegment.text
                : activeSegment.translated_text ||
                  activeSegment.text ||
                  activeSegment.original_text}
            </Text>
            {subMode === 'bilingual' &&
              activeSegment.original_text &&
              activeSegment.original_text.trim() !==
                (
                  activeSegment.translated_text ||
                  activeSegment.text ||
                  ''
                ).trim() && (
                <Text style={styles.ccOverlaySubText} numberOfLines={1} ellipsizeMode="tail">
                  {activeSegment.original_text}
                </Text>
              )}
          </View>
        </View>
      )}

      {/* Lớp phủ Trắc nghiệm tương tác theo mốc thời gian */}
      {activeCheckpointQuiz && (
        <InVideoQuizOverlay
          activeCheckpointQuiz={activeCheckpointQuiz}
          formatTime={formatTime}
          handleResumeFromQuiz={handleResumeFromQuiz}
          handleSelectQuizOption={handleSelectQuizOption}
          styles={styles}
        />
      )}
    </View>
  );
}

export const resolveAvatarUrl = (value) => {
  if (!value) return null;
  if (
    value.startsWith('http') ||
    value.startsWith('data:') ||
    value.startsWith('blob:') ||
    value.startsWith('file:') ||
    value.startsWith('content:')
  ) {
    return value;
  }
  return LEGACY_AVATAR_MAP[value] || null;
};

export const resolveAvatarIcon = (value) => {
  if (!value) return null;
  return resolveAvatarUrl(value) || value;
};

// Cấu hình Hệ thống Phân bố Cấp bậc & Thăng hạng học viên

    desc: 'Kiến thức vững vàng, phản xạ xuất sắc',
  },
  {
    id: 'master',
    tier: 5,
    name: 'Huyền Thoại Tri Thức',
    shortName: 'Huyền Thoại',
    icon: 'crown',
    minExp: 1500,
    color: '#a855f7',
    bg: 'rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.12)',
    desc: 'Đỉnh cao học giả, hoàn thiện toàn diện',
  },
];

export const getRankDetails = (exp = 0) => {
  let currentIdx = 0;
  for (let i = RANKS.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    if (exp >= RANKS[i].minExp) {
      currentIdx = i;
      break;
    }
  }
  const current = RANKS[currentIdx];
  const next = RANKS[currentIdx + 1] || null;

  let progressPercent = 100;
  let expNeeded = 0;

  if (next) {
    const tierExp = exp - current.minExp;
    const tierTarget = next.minExp - current.minExp;
    progressPercent = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, Math.round((tierExp / tierTarget) * 100)));
    expNeeded = Math.max(0, next.minExp - exp);
  }

  return { current, next, currentIdx, progressPercent, expNeeded, totalExp: exp };
};

import React from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  Modal,
  ScrollView,
  TextInput,
  TouchableOpacity,
  Image,
  ActivityIndicator,
} from 'react-native';
import { colors } from '../../../constants/theme';
import { PRESET_AVATARS, resolveAvatarUrl, resolveAvatarIcon } from '../constants/profileData';
import Icon from '../../ui/Icon';
import styles from '../styles/profileStyles';

/**
 * Modal Chỉnh sửa hồ sơ, Avatar (từ thiết bị hoặc preset AI), và Quy trình đổi Email qua OTP
 */
export default function EditProfileModal({
  visible,
  onClose,
  user,
  selectedAvatar,
  onSelectAvatar,
  onPickAvatarFromDevice,
  editDisplayName,
  onChangeDisplayName,
  editEmail,
  onChangeEmail,
  editEmailOtpStep = 1,
  setEditEmailOtpStep,
  pendingChangeEmail,
  emailChangeOtp,
  onChangeEmailOtp,
  editLoading = false,
  editMsg = '',
  editError = '',
  onSaveProfile,
  onConfirmEmailOtp,
}) {
  return (
    <Modal
      visible={visible}
      transparent
      animationType="fade"
      onRequestClose={onClose}
    >
      <View style={styles.modalOverlay}>
        <View style={[styles.modalCard, { maxHeight: '90%' }]}>
          <View style={styles.modalHeader}>
            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, flex: 1 }}>
              <Icon
                name={editEmailOtpStep === 2 ? 'lock' : 'manageAccounts'}
                size={22}
                color={colors.coral}
              />
              <Text style={styles.modalTitle}>
                {editEmailOtpStep === 2 ? 'Xác Thực Đổi Email' : 'Chỉnh Sửa Hồ Sơ & Avatar'}
              </Text>
            </View>
            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={onClose}
              style={styles.closeBtn}
              activeOpacity={0.7}
            >
              <Icon name="close" size={18} color={colors.slate} />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          <ScrollView showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false} style={{ marginVertical: 6 }}>
            {editError ? (
              <View style={styles.errorBox}>
                <Text style={styles.errorText}>{editError}</Text>
              </View>
            ) : null}
            {editMsg ? (
              <View style={styles.successBox}>
                <Text style={styles.successText}>{editMsg}</Text>
              </View>
            ) : null}

            {editEmailOtpStep === 1 ? (
              <>
                {/* Phần Avatar xem trước & Đổi Avatar */}
                <Text style={styles.editSectionLabel}>ẢNH ĐẠI DIỆN HỌC VIÊN</Text>
                <View style={styles.avatarPreviewRow}>
                  <View style={styles.avatarPreviewBig}>
                    {(() => {
                      const avatarUri = resolveAvatarUrl(selectedAvatar) || (selectedAvatar && (selectedAvatar.startsWith('http') || selectedAvatar.startsWith('data:') || selectedAvatar.startsWith('blob:') || selectedAvatar.startsWith('file:') || selectedAvatar.startsWith('content:')) ? selectedAvatar : null);
                      if (avatarUri) {
                        return <Image source={{ uri: avatarUri }} style={styles.avatarBigImage} />;
                      }
                      return (
                        <Text style={styles.avatarBigText}>
                          {(editDisplayName || user?.email || 'U')[0].toUpperCase()}
                        </Text>
                      );
                    })()}
                  </View>

                  <View style={styles.avatarActionCol}>
                    <TouchableOpacity
                      style={styles.uploadAvatarBtn}
                      onPress={onPickAvatarFromDevice}
                      activeOpacity={0.8}
                    >
                      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
                        <Icon name="folderOpen" size={16} color={colors.violet} />
                        <Text style={styles.uploadAvatarBtnText}>Chọn ảnh từ thiết bị</Text>
                      </View>
                    </TouchableOpacity>

                    {selectedAvatar && (
                      <TouchableOpacity
                        style={styles.removeAvatarBtn}
                        onPress={() => onSelectAvatar(null)}
                        activeOpacity={0.7}
                      >
                        <Text style={styles.removeAvatarBtnText}>Xóa ảnh (Dùng chữ cái)</Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    )}
                  </View>
                </View>

                {/* Bộ sưu tập Avatar Preset */}
                <Text style={styles.editSectionSubLabel}>Hoặc chọn nhanh Avatar phong cách AI:</Text>
                <View style={styles.presetAvatarGrid}>
                  {PRESET_AVATARS.map((item) => {
                    const isSelected = selectedAvatar === item.url || selectedAvatar === item.id;
                    return (
                      <TouchableOpacity
                        key={item.id}
                        style={[styles.presetAvatarItem, isSelected && styles.presetAvatarItemActive]}
                        onPress={() => onSelectAvatar(item.url)}
                        activeOpacity={0.75}
                      >
                        <View style={styles.presetAvatarImgWrap}>
                          <Image
                            source={{ uri: item.url }}
                            style={styles.presetAvatarImg}
                          />
                          {isSelected ? (
                            <View style={styles.presetAvatarSelectedBadge}>
                              <Icon name="check" size={9} color="#FFFFFF" />
                            </View>
                          ) : null}
                        </View>
                        <Text
                          style={[styles.presetAvatarLabel, isSelected && styles.presetAvatarLabelActive]}
                          numberOfLines={1}
                        >
                          {item.label}
                        </Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    );
                  })}
                </View>

                {/* Phần Tên hiển thị */}
                <View style={{ marginTop: 14 }}>
                  <Text style={[styles.editSectionLabel, { marginTop: 0 }]}>TÊN HIỂN THỊ HỌC VIÊN</Text>
                  <View style={styles.inputWrap}>
                    <Icon name="badge" size={16} color={colors.muted} style={{ marginRight: 8 }} />
                    <TextInput
                      style={styles.textInput}
                      placeholder="Nhập tên hiển thị mới"
                      placeholderTextColor={colors.textMuted}
                      value={editDisplayName}
                      onChangeText={onChangeDisplayName}
                      autoCapitalize="words"
                    />
                  </View>
                </View>

                {/* Phần Email */}
                <View style={{ marginTop: 14 }}>
                  <Text style={[styles.editSectionLabel, { marginTop: 0 }]}>EMAIL TÀI KHOẢN (LIÊN KẾT HỆ THỐNG)</Text>
                  <View style={styles.inputWrap}>
                    <Icon name="mail" size={16} color={colors.muted} style={{ marginRight: 8 }} />
                    <TextInput
                      style={styles.textInput}
                      placeholder="Nhập địa chỉ email mới"
                      placeholderTextColor={colors.textMuted}
                      value={editEmail}
                      onChangeText={onChangeEmail}
                      keyboardType="email-address"
                      autoCapitalize="none"
                      autoCorrect={false}
                    />
                  </View>
                  <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'flex-start', gap: 4 }}>
                    <Icon name="bulb" size={13} color={colors.coral} style={{ marginTop: 2 }} />
                    <Text style={[styles.emailHintText, { flex: 1 }]}>
                      Khi đổi sang email mới, hệ thống sẽ gửi mã OTP 6 số để xác thực trước khi cập nhật.
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                </View>
              </>
            ) : (
              /* Bước 2: Xác thực mã OTP đổi Email */
              <View style={styles.otpStepContainer}>
                <View style={styles.otpInfoBadge}>
                  <Icon name="mail" size={22} color={colors.violet} />
                  <Text style={styles.otpInfoBadgeText}>
                    Mã xác nhận 6 số đã được gửi đến email mới:
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={styles.otpTargetEmailText}>{pendingChangeEmail}</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.otpInfoBadgeText, { marginTop: 6, fontSize: 10.5 }]}>
                    Vui lòng nhập mã OTP để hoàn tất thay đổi email tài khoản.
                  </Text>
                </View>

                <Text style={styles.otpInputTitle}>NHẬP MÃ OTP (6 CHỮ SỐ)</Text>
                <View style={styles.otpInputWrapper}>
                  <TextInput
                    style={styles.otpCodeInput}
                    placeholder="••••••"
                    placeholderTextColor={colors.muted}
                    value={emailChangeOtp}
                    onChangeText={onChangeEmailOtp}
                    keyboardType="numeric"
                    maxLength={6}
                    textAlign="center"
                    autoFocus
                  />
                </View>

                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={styles.resendOtpBtn}
                  onPress={onSaveProfile}
                  disabled={editLoading}
                  activeOpacity={0.7}
                >
                  <Text style={styles.resendOtpBtnText}>Chưa nhận được mã? Gửi lại OTP</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>
            )}
          </ScrollView>

          {/* Hàng nút hành động */}
          <View style={styles.editModalBtnRow}>
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.editModalCancelBtn}
              onPress={() => {
                if (editEmailOtpStep === 2) {
                  setEditEmailOtpStep(1);
                } else {
                  onClose();
                }
              }}
              activeOpacity={0.7}
            >
              <Text style={styles.editModalCancelBtnText}>
                {editEmailOtpStep === 2 ? '‹ Quay lại' : 'Hủy'}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[styles.editModalSubmitBtn, editLoading && styles.editModalSubmitBtnDisabled]}
              onPress={editEmailOtpStep === 2 ? onConfirmEmailOtp : onSaveProfile}
              disabled={editLoading}
              activeOpacity={0.8}
            >
              {editLoading ? (
                <ActivityIndicator color="#fff" size="small" />
              ) : (
                <Text style={styles.editModalSubmitBtnText}>
                  {editEmailOtpStep === 2 ? 'Xác nhận OTP' : 'Lưu thay đổi'}
                </Text>
              )}
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>
      </View>
    </Modal>
  );
}

export { default as EditProfileModal } from './EditProfileModal';

FILE: src/mobile-app/src/components/profile/sections/AuthSection.js
